── Сообщение #2003 (id=2629) ──
Чат: Rastafari WORLDWIDE 🧮 chat
Отправитель: Неизвестный
Дата: 14.08.2026, 16:12:08
Текст:
Zapscape is presented as an easy-to-use educational program about a serious virtualization security issue called a KVM escape.
Normally, a virtual machine is isolated from the real computer, much like a guest inside a locked room. A KVM escape would mean that software running inside the virtual machine finds a way out of that isolation and interacts with the host computer.
The text claims that Zapscape demonstrates this concept using a vulnerability identified as CVE-2026-64561. It is advertised as portable, requiring no programming knowledge, and designed for security education and research.
